{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"lighters","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"a lighter of lamps","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A person who lights (“sets fire to or ignites”) a thing."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Cigarette in mouth, he clutched his pockets in search of a lighter.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An electrical or mechanical device, usually handheld and reusable, used to light things; especially, a device which creates a small flame to light cigarettes, stoves, etc."],"synonyms":[{"tags":["dated"],"word":"briquet"}]},{"glosses":["A piece of flammable material which is set on fire and used to light something else; especially, a piece of folded or twisted paper used in this way to light a pipe."],"tags":["archaic"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈlaɪtə/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈlɑj-/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation","British","Southern","Standard"]},{"ipa":"/ˈlaɪtəɹ/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"[-ɾəɹ]","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈlɑetə/","tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/ˈlʌɪtəɾ/","tags":["Scotland"]}],"upstream_line":242326,"upstream_offset":1202755711,"upstream_record_sha256":"dcd1e3c794076ba19fbd2235075f4b97297b4f32f962604878e66888916d173f","word":"lighter"}
